We are now ready to give the proof of Lemma 6.2.
As we noted, this theorem is an extension of Theorem 3.3 of [19]
and gives a further error estimate. Because the definition of G~\widetilde{G}
is different, the proof is modified by applying Hölder’s inequality.



Proof of Lemma 6.2.



To apply Lemma A.1 by localizing in space, finally to
obtain the estimate in terms of a spatial integral and also to match
with the scale ℓ\ell in G~\widetilde{G}, we divide 𝕋Nd{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d} into disjoint
boxes {Λℓ,a}a\{\Lambda_{\ell,a}\}_{a} with
side length ℓ∗=2​ℓ+1\ell_{*}=2\ell+1 and centered at a∈ℓ∗​𝕋N/ℓ∗d={ℓ∗,2​ℓ∗,…,N}da\in\ell_{*}{\mathbb{T}}_{N/\ell_{*}}^{d}=\{\ell_{*},2\ell_{*},\ldots,N\}^{d}, assuming N/ℓ∗∈ℕN/\ell_{*}\in{\mathbb{N}} for simplicity
(if not, we may make the side lengths of some of the boxes smaller
or larger by 11).




Since x∈𝕋Ndx\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d} is uniquely decomposed as x=y+ax=y+a with
y∈Λ⁡(ℓ)=Λℓ,0y\in\Lambda(\ell)=\Lambda_{\ell,0} and a∈ℓ∗​𝕋N/ℓ∗da\in\ell_{*}{\mathbb{T}}_{N/\ell_{*}}^{d}
(we write a⁡(x)a(x) for aa)
according to this division, writing the sum and the product in
a∈ℓ∗​𝕋N/ℓ∗da\in\ell_{*}{\mathbb{T}}_{N/\ell_{*}}^{d} by ∑a∗\sum_{a}^{*} and ∏a∗\prod_{a}^{*},
respectively, we have





I:=\displaystyle I:=
Eνλ⁡(⋅),FN​[exp⁡G~​(η)]\displaystyle E^{\nu_{\lambda(\cdot),F}^{N}}[\exp\widetilde{G}(\eta)]




=\displaystyle=
Zλ⁡(⋅),F,N−1EνN[exp{∑y∈Λ⁡(ℓ)∑a[G(y+a)/N,η¯y+aℓ)∗+λ(a/N)η¯y+aℓ]+R(η)}]\displaystyle Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}^{-1}E^{\nu^{N}}\bigg[\exp\bigg\{\sum_{y\in\Lambda(\ell)}\sum_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}\big[G(y+a)/N,\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell})+\lambda(a/N)\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell}\big]+R(\eta)\bigg\}\bigg]




=\displaystyle=
Zλ⁡(⋅),F,N−1EνN[∏y∈Λ⁡(ℓ)exp{∑a[G((y+a)/N,η¯y+aℓ)+λ(a/N)η¯y+aℓ]∗}⋅eR⁡(η)]\displaystyle Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}^{-1}E^{\nu^{N}}\Bigg[\prod_{y\in\Lambda(\ell)}\exp\bigg\{\sum_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}\big[G((y+a)/N,\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell})+\lambda(a/N)\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell}\big]\bigg\}\cdot e^{R(\eta)}\Bigg]




≤\displaystyle\leq
e‖R‖∞Zλ⁡(⋅),F,N−1∏y∈Λ⁡(ℓ)EνN[exp{ℓ∗d∑a[G((y+a)/N,η¯y+aℓ)+λ(a/N)η¯y+aℓ]∗}]1/ℓ∗d,\displaystyle e^{\|R\|_{\infty}}Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}^{-1}\prod_{y\in\Lambda(\ell)}E^{\nu^{N}}\Bigg[\exp\bigg\{\ell_{*}^{d}\sum_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}\big[G((y+a)/N,\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell})+\lambda(a/N)\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell}\big]\bigg\}\Bigg]^{1/\ell_{*}^{d}},



where R⁡(η)=R1+R2R(\eta)=R_{1}+R_{2} and






R1=1N​∑x∈𝕋Nd(∂λ⁡(x/N),τx​F​(η)),\displaystyle R_{1}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\left(\partial\lambda(x/N),\tau_{x}F(\eta)\right),





R2=∑x∈𝕋Nd(λ⁡(x/N)​ηx−λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N)​η¯xℓ).\displaystyle R_{2}=\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\big(\lambda(x/N)\eta_{x}-\lambda(a(x)/N)\bar{\eta}_{x}^{\ell}\big).



For the last line, we have first estimated eR≤e‖R‖∞e^{R}\leq e^{\|R\|_{\infty}} and then
applied Hölder’s inequality under νN\nu^{N}.
Two error terms R1R_{1} and R2R_{2} are estimated as follows:




(B.10)

|R1​(η)|≤Nd−1​‖∂λ‖∞​‖F‖∞,|R2​(η)|≤ℓ∗​Nd−1​‖∂λ‖∞.\displaystyle|R_{1}(\eta)|\leq N^{d-1}\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty}\|F\|_{\infty},\quad|R_{2}(\eta)|\leq\ell_{*}N^{d-1}\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty}.



In fact, for R2R_{2}, note that the sum of the second term of R2R_{2} can be rewritten as





∑x∈𝕋Ndλ⁡(a⁡(x)/N)​ℓ∗−d​∑z∈Λ⁡(ℓ)ηx+z=∑x′∈𝕋Ndηx′​ℓ∗−d​∑z∈Λ⁡(ℓ)λ⁡(a⁡(x′−z)/N).\displaystyle\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\lambda(a(x)/N)\ell_{*}^{-d}\sum_{z\in\Lambda(\ell)}\eta_{x+z}=\sum_{x^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\eta_{x^{\prime}}\ell_{*}^{-d}\sum_{z\in\Lambda(\ell)}\lambda(a(x^{\prime}-z)/N).






Since η¯y+aℓ\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell} are functions of η|Λℓ,y+a\eta\big|_{\Lambda_{\ell,y+a}}, they
are independent for different aa under νN\nu^{N}. Therefore, the last
expectation in the bound for II is factorized and
is rewritten as






EνN[exp{ℓ∗d∑a[G((y+a)/N,η¯y+aℓ)+λ(a/N)η¯y+aℓ]∗}]\displaystyle E^{\nu^{N}}\Bigg[\exp\bigg\{\ell_{*}^{d}\sum_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}\big[G((y+a)/N,\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell})+\lambda(a/N)\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell}\big]\bigg\}\Bigg]





=∏aEνN∗[exp{ℓ∗d[G((y+a)/N,η¯y+aℓ)+λ(a/N)η¯y+aℓ]}]\displaystyle=\prod_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}E^{\nu^{N}}\Bigg[\exp\bigg\{\ell_{*}^{d}\big[G((y+a)/N,\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell})+\lambda(a/N)\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell}\big]\bigg\}\Bigg]





=∏a(ZΛℓ,y+a,λ⁡(a/N)⋅2−ℓ∗d)∗EνΛℓ,y+a,λ⁡(a/N)[exp{ℓ∗dG((y+a)/N,η¯y+aℓ)}].\displaystyle=\prod_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}\big(Z_{\Lambda_{\ell,y+a},\lambda(a/N)}\cdot 2^{-\ell_{*}^{d}}\big)E^{\nu_{\Lambda_{\ell,y+a},\lambda(a/N)}}\bigg[\exp\big\{\ell_{*}^{d}G((y+a)/N,\bar{\eta}_{y+a}^{\ell})\big\}\bigg].



In the second line, νN\nu^{N} can be replaced by
ν¯1/2⊗Λℓ,y+a\bar{\nu}_{1/2}^{\otimes\Lambda_{\ell,y+a}}
and, recalling (B.7), we obtain the third line.
Thus, noting the shift-invariance of ZΛ,λZ_{\Lambda,\lambda} and νΛ,λ\nu_{\Lambda,\lambda} in Λ\Lambda,
and ∏y∏a∗=∏x\prod_{y}\prod_{a}^{*}=\prod_{x}, we have





I≤e‖R‖∞​Zλ⁡(⋅),F,N−1\displaystyle I\leq e^{\|R\|_{\infty}}Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}^{-1}
∏aZΛ⁡(ℓ),λ⁡(a/N)∗⋅2−ℓ∗d×∏x∈𝕋NdEνΛ⁡(ℓ),λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N)[exp{ℓ∗dG(x/N,η¯0ℓ)}]1/ℓ∗d,\displaystyle\prod_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}Z_{\Lambda(\ell),\lambda(a/N)}\cdot 2^{-\ell_{*}^{d}}\times\prod_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}E^{\nu_{\Lambda(\ell),\lambda(a(x)/N)}}\bigg[\exp\big\{\ell_{*}^{d}G(x/N,\bar{\eta}_{0}^{\ell})\big\}\bigg]^{1/\ell_{*}^{d}},



and therefore,




(B.11)

N−d​log⁡I≤\displaystyle N^{-d}\log I\leq
N−d∥R∥∞+N−dlog(Zλ⁡(⋅),F,N−1∏aZΛ⁡(ℓ),λ⁡(a/N)∗⋅2−ℓ∗d)\displaystyle N^{-d}\|R\|_{\infty}+N^{-d}\log\Big(Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}^{-1}\prod_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}Z_{\Lambda(\ell),\lambda(a/N)}\cdot 2^{-\ell_{*}^{d}}\Big)





+N−d∑x∈𝕋Ndℓ∗−dlogEνΛ⁡(ℓ),λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N)[exp{ℓ∗dG(x/N,η¯0ℓ)}].\displaystyle+N^{-d}\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\ell_{*}^{-d}\log E^{\nu_{\Lambda(\ell),\lambda(a(x)/N)}}\bigg[\exp\big\{\ell_{*}^{d}G(x/N,\bar{\eta}_{0}^{\ell})\big\}\bigg].






For the partition functions, recalling that






ZΛ,λ⋅2−|Λ|=Eν¯1/2⊗Λ​[exp⁡{λ​∑x∈Ληx}],λ∈ℝ,\displaystyle Z_{\Lambda,\lambda}\cdot 2^{-|\Lambda|}=E^{\bar{\nu}_{1/2}^{\otimes\Lambda}}\Big[\exp\Big\{\lambda\sum_{x\in\Lambda}\eta_{x}\Big\}\Big],\quad\lambda\in{\mathbb{R}},





Zλ⁡(⋅),F,N=EνN​[exp⁡{∑x∈𝕋Ndλ⁡(x/N)​ηx+R1​(η)}],\displaystyle Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}=E^{\nu^{N}}\Big[\exp\Big\{\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\lambda(x/N)\eta_{x}+R_{1}(\eta)\Big\}\Big],



and changing Λ⁡(ℓ)\Lambda(\ell) to Λℓ,a\Lambda_{\ell,a} in ZΛ⁡(ℓ),λ⁡(a/N)Z_{\Lambda(\ell),\lambda(a/N)}, we obtain





∏a∗ZΛℓ,a,λ⁡(a/N)⋅2−ℓ∗d=Zλ~​(⋅),0,N,\displaystyle\prod_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}Z_{\Lambda_{\ell,a},\lambda(a/N)}\cdot 2^{-\ell_{*}^{d}}=Z_{\widetilde{\lambda}(\cdot),0,N},



where λ~​(v)=∑a∗λ⁡(a/N)​1Λℓ,a​(N​v)\widetilde{\lambda}(v)=\sum_{a}^{*}\lambda(a/N)1_{\Lambda_{\ell,a}}(Nv)
is a step function on 𝕋d{\mathbb{T}}^{d} and F=0F=0 on the right-hand side.
To replace the step function λ~​(⋅)\widetilde{\lambda}(\cdot) with the original function
λ⁡(⋅)\lambda(\cdot), note that





Zλ~​(⋅),0,NZλ⁡(⋅),0,N\displaystyle\frac{Z_{\widetilde{\lambda}(\cdot),0,N}}{Z_{\lambda(\cdot),0,N}}
=EνN​[exp⁡{∑x∈𝕋Ndλ~​(x/N)​ηx}]EνN​[exp⁡{∑x∈𝕋Ndλ⁡(x/N)​ηx}]\displaystyle=\frac{E^{\nu_{N}}[\exp\{\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\widetilde{\lambda}(x/N)\eta_{x}\}]}{E^{\nu_{N}}[\exp\{\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\lambda(x/N)\eta_{x}\}]}





=EνN​[exp⁡{∑x∈𝕋Ndλ⁡(x/N)​ηx+∑x∈𝕋Nd(λ~​(x/N)−λ⁡(x/N))​ηx}]EνN​[exp⁡{∑x∈𝕋Ndλ⁡(x/N)​ηx}]\displaystyle=\frac{E^{\nu_{N}}[\exp\{\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\lambda(x/N)\eta_{x}+\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}(\widetilde{\lambda}(x/N)-\lambda(x/N))\eta_{x}\}]}{E^{\nu_{N}}[\exp\{\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\lambda(x/N)\eta_{x}\}]}





≤eℓ​Nd−1​‖∂λ‖∞.\displaystyle\leq e^{\ell N^{d-1}\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty}}.



To compare two partition functions with and without FF, we have





Zλ⁡(⋅),0,NZλ⁡(⋅),F,N≤e‖R1‖∞.\displaystyle\frac{Z_{\lambda(\cdot),0,N}}{Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}}\leq e^{\|R_{1}\|_{\infty}}.



Summarizing these and noting (B.10), we have shown for
first two terms in (B.11) that




(B.12)

N−d​‖R‖∞\displaystyle N^{-d}\|R\|_{\infty}
+N−dlog(Zλ⁡(⋅),F,N−1∏aZΛ⁡(ℓ),λ⁡(a/N)∗⋅2−ℓ∗d)\displaystyle+N^{-d}\log\Big(Z_{\lambda(\cdot),F,N}^{-1}\prod_{a}\!{}^{{}^{*}}Z_{\Lambda(\ell),\lambda(a/N)}\cdot 2^{-\ell_{*}^{d}}\Big)





≤C​N−1​‖∂λ‖∞​(ℓ+‖F‖∞).\displaystyle\leq CN^{-1}\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty}(\ell+\|F\|_{\infty}).






For the last term of (B.11),
one can apply Lemma A.1 to see that





N−d\displaystyle N^{-d}
∑x∈𝕋Ndℓ∗−d​log⁡EνΛ⁡(ℓ),λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N)​[exp⁡{ℓ∗d​G​(x/N,η¯0ℓ)}]\displaystyle\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\ell_{*}^{-d}\log E^{\nu_{\Lambda(\ell),\lambda(a(x)/N)}}\Big[\exp\big\{\ell_{*}^{d}G(x/N,\bar{\eta}_{0}^{\ell})\big\}\Big]





=N−d​∑x∈𝕋Nd{supρ∈[0,1]{G⁡(x/N,ρ)−I⁡(ρ,λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N))}+Qℓ​(λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N),G⁡(x/N,⋅))},\displaystyle=N^{-d}\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\bigg\{\sup_{\rho\in[0,1]}\{G(x/N,\rho)-I(\rho;\lambda(a(x)/N))\}+Q_{\ell}(\lambda(a(x)/N),G(x/N,\cdot))\bigg\},



and




(B.13)

|Qℓ​(λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N),G⁡(x/N,⋅))|≤Cℓd​(log⁡ℓ+‖λ‖∞+‖∂ρG1‖∞+‖∂ρG2‖∞).|Q_{\ell}(\lambda(a(x)/N),G(x/N,\cdot))|\leq\frac{C}{\ell^{d}}(\log\ell+\|\lambda\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{\rho}G_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{\rho}G_{2}\|_{\infty}).



Moreover, one can estimate




(B.14)

N−d∑x∈𝕋Ndsupρ∈[0,1]{G(x/N,ρ)−I(ρ;λ(a(x)/N))}≤supρ:step on ​𝕋d𝔾(ρ)+Qℓ,N,\displaystyle N^{-d}\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}\sup_{\rho\in[0,1]}\{G(x/N,\rho)-I(\rho;\lambda(a(x)/N))\}\leq\sup_{\rho:\text{step on }{\mathbb{T}}^{d}}\mathbb{G}(\rho)+Q_{\ell,N},



where the supremum on the right-hand side is taken over all
step functions ρ⁡(v)\rho(v) on 𝕋d{\mathbb{T}}^{d},




(B.15)


Qℓ,N≡Qℓ,N​(λ,G)=2​ℓN​(‖∂λ‖∞+‖∂vG1‖∞+‖∂vG2‖∞)\displaystyle Q_{\ell,N}\equiv Q_{\ell,N}(\lambda;G)=\frac{2\ell}{N}\,\Big(\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{v}G_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{v}G_{2}\|_{\infty}\Big)



and





𝔾⁡(ρ)=∫𝕋d{G⁡(v,ρ⁡(v))−I⁡(ρ⁡(v),λ⁡(v))}​𝑑v,\mathbb{G}(\rho)=\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{d}}\{G(v,\rho(v))-I(\rho(v);\lambda(v))\}\,dv,



for ρ∈L1​(𝕋d,[0,1])\rho\in L^{1}({\mathbb{T}}^{d};[0,1]).
Indeed, we first replace G⁡(x/N,ρ)G(x/N,\rho) in (B.14) by G⁡(a⁡(x)/N,ρ)G(a(x)/N,\rho)
with an error within ℓ​N−1​(‖∂vG1‖∞+‖∂vG2‖∞)\ell N^{-1}(\|\partial_{v}G_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{v}G_{2}\|_{\infty}), and then the supremum is attained at
some ρ⁡(a⁡(x)/N)\rho(a(x)/N) for each a=a⁡(x)a=a(x). This defines a step function
ρ⁡(v)=∑a∗ρ⁡(a/N)​1Λℓ,a​(N​v)\rho(v)=\sum_{a}^{*}\rho(a/N)1_{\Lambda_{\ell,a}}(Nv), and leads to the
functional 𝔾⁡(ρ)\mathbb{G}(\rho), but with an discretization also for
GG and λ\lambda in the variable vv. The error of removing these discretization
in GG and λ\lambda is estimated by using






sup|v1−v2|≤ℓ/N|λ⁡(v1)−λ⁡(v2)|≤ℓ​N−1​‖∂λ‖∞,\displaystyle\sup_{|v_{1}-v_{2}|\leq\ell/N}|\lambda(v_{1})-\lambda(v_{2})|\leq\ell N^{-1}\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty},





sup|v1−v2|≤ℓ/Nsupρ∈[0,1]|G⁡(v1,ρ)−G⁡(v2,ρ)|≤ℓ​N−1​(‖∂vG1‖∞+‖∂vG2‖∞),\displaystyle\sup_{|v_{1}-v_{2}|\leq\ell/N}\sup_{\rho\in[0,1]}|G(v_{1},\rho)-G(v_{2},\rho)|\leq\ell N^{-1}\,\big(\|\partial_{v}G_{1}\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{v}G_{2}\|_{\infty}\big),





|∫𝕋dp⁡(λ⁡(v))​𝑑v−N−d​∑x∈𝕋Ndp⁡(λ⁡(a⁡(x)/N))|≤ℓ​N−1​‖p′‖∞​‖∂λ‖∞≤ℓ​N−1​‖∂λ‖∞,\displaystyle\bigg|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{d}}p(\lambda(v))\,dv-N^{-d}\sum_{x\in{\mathbb{T}}_{N}^{d}}p(\lambda(a(x)/N))\bigg|\leq\ell N^{-1}\|p^{\prime}\|_{\infty}\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty}\leq\ell N^{-1}\|\partial\lambda\|_{\infty},



and obtain (B.14) with Qℓ,NQ_{\ell,N} given by (B.15).




Since 𝔾⁡(ρ)\mathbb{G}(\rho) is continuous in
ρ∈L1​(𝕋d,[0,1])\rho\in L^{1}({\mathbb{T}}^{d};[0,1]) and C⁡(𝕋d,[0,1])C({\mathbb{T}}^{d};[0,1]) is dense
in L1​(𝕋d,[0,1])L^{1}({\mathbb{T}}^{d};[0,1]), we obtain the conclusion of Lemma 6.2
from (B.12), (B.13), (B.14) and (B.15).
∎





Finally, we make a small comment on other application of
Lemma 6.2. Theorem 1.1 implies
with the help of Chebyshev’s inequality





P⁡(|⟨ρN​(t),ϕ⟩−⟨ρK​(t),ϕ⟩|>N−p)≤C​‖ϕ‖Hα​(𝕋d)2​N−κ+2​p,p∈(0,κ/2).P\big(|\langle\rho^{N}(t),\phi\rangle-\langle\rho_{K}(t),\phi\rangle|>N^{-p}\big)\leq C\|\phi\|_{H^{\alpha}({\mathbb{T}}^{d})}^{2}N^{-\kappa+2p},\quad p\in(0,\kappa/2).






This decay estimate of the probability follows also from Lemma 6.2,
Theorem 2.1 and the entropy inequality. Indeed, set





𝒜N,ϕ,λ⁡(⋅),p={η∈𝒳N;|⟨ρN,ϕ⟩−⟨ρ¯​(λ⁡(⋅)),ϕ⟩|>N−p}.\mathcal{A}_{N,\phi,\lambda(\cdot),p}=\big\{\eta\in\mathcal{X}_{N};\,|\langle\rho^{N},\phi\rangle-\langle\bar{\rho}(\lambda(\cdot)),\phi\rangle|>N^{-p}\big\}.



Then, Lemma 6.2 shows the moderate deviation type upper bound:





Pψλ⁡(⋅),FNN​(𝒜N,ϕ,λ⁡(⋅),p)≤C1​e−c​Nd−2​p,P^{\psi_{\lambda(\cdot),F_{N}}^{N}}(\mathcal{A}_{N,\phi,\lambda(\cdot),p})\leq C_{1}e^{-cN^{d-2p}},



for some C1,c>0C_{1},c>0,
taking F=FN,ℓ=ℓ⁡(N)=Na2F=F_{N},\ell=\ell(N)=N^{a_{2}} as in Section 7.2 and
G⁡(v,ρ)=ϕ⁡(v)​{ρ−ρ¯​(λ⁡(v))}−N−p/2G(v,\rho)=\phi(v)\{\rho-\bar{\rho}(\lambda(v))\}-N^{-p}/2 in Lemma 6.2.
Therefore, by the entropy inequality and Theorem 2.1:
H⁡(ft|ψt)≤C​Nd−κH(f_{t}|\psi_{t})\leq CN^{d-\kappa}, we obtain





Pft​(𝒜N,ϕ,λK​(t,⋅),p)\displaystyle P^{f_{t}}(\mathcal{A}_{N,\phi,\lambda_{K}(t,\cdot),p})
≤log⁡2+H⁡(ft|ψt)log⁡{1+1/Pψt​(𝒜N,ϕ,λK​(t,⋅),p)}\displaystyle\leq\frac{\log 2+H(f_{t}|\psi_{t})}{\log\{1+1/P^{\psi_{t}}(\mathcal{A}_{N,\phi,\lambda_{K}(t,\cdot),p})\}}





≤log⁡2+C​Nd−κlog⁡(1+ec​Nd−2​p/C1)≤C2​N−κ+2​p.\displaystyle\leq\frac{\log 2+CN^{d-\kappa}}{\log(1+e^{cN^{d-2p}}/C_{1})}\leq C_{2}N^{-\kappa+2p}.

Instructions for reporting errors
    
We are continuing to improve HTML versions of papers, and your feedback helps enhance accessibility and mobile
      support. To report errors in the HTML that will help us improve conversion and rendering, choose any of the
      methods listed below:
    
      
Click the "Report Issue" (
            
          ) button, located in the page header.
    
    
Tip: You can select the relevant text first, to include it in your report.
    
Our team has already identified the following issues. We appreciate your time reviewing and reporting rendering errors we
      may not have found yet. Your efforts will help us improve the HTML versions for all readers, because disability
      should not be a barrier to accessing research. Thank you for your continued support in championing open access for
      all.
    
Have a free development cycle? Help support accessibility at arXiv! Our collaborators at LaTeXML maintain a list of packages that need conversion, and welcome developer contributions.
